# FarmMate CIB Knowledge Base (Products x Government-Approved Uses)
Source: Central Insecticide Board (CIB&RC) 'Major Uses of Pesticides', registered under Insecticides Act 1968, data as on 31/03/2026. For guidance only, not for legal purposes.
Dosage figures below are per HECTARE as published by CIB — divide by ~2.47 to convert to per-ACRE for farmer-facing advice.
